# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-17T03:41:26+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

## Wider reading

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Mon, 16 Nov 2026 18:42:00 -0300 — EBC inaugura nova sede em São Paulo e amplia estrutura de produção
  A Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC) inaugurou oficialmente, nesta segunda-feira (16), sua nova sede em São Paulo, localizada na Avenida Prestes Maia, nº 733, na região central da capital paulista. A cerimônia marcou o início de uma nova etapa da atuação da empresa na maior cidade do Brasil, com uma estrutura modernizada, novos espaços de produção e perspectivas de fortalecimento da infraestrutura de transmissão. Notícias relacionadas: EBC recebe selo Diamante em avaliação de transparência pública. EBC conquista mais R$ 23 milhões para investimento na TV 3.0 no Brasil. Na ocasião, houve o descerramento da placa de inauguração da nova sede, um tour de apresentação das novas instalações e a apresentação da banda Som dos Quintos. Participaram da solenidade a diretora-presidente, Antonia Pell
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/geral/noticia/2026-11/ebc-inaugura-nova-sede-em-sao-paulo-e-amplia-estrutura-de-producao

- Mon, 16 Nov 2026 21:11:30 +0000 — This Supernova is 50 Million Light Years Away, But That's Close Enough For Astronomers
  A new supernova has been detected in a spiral galaxy about 50 million light years away. That's close enough for detatiled follow-up observations, which have already begun. It's a type Ia supernova, meaning it's an exploding white dwarf rather than a massive star ending its life in a core-collapse detonation. Astronomers will watch as its light fades and changes over the next days, months, and years to track the changes and learn more about these standard candles. It's close enough for astrophotographers to get in on the action, too.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/this-supernova-is-50-million-light-years-away-but-thats-close-enough-for-astronomers
- Mon, 16 Nov 2026 19:30:47 +0000 — This Rare Binary Star Emits Radio Pulses Every Few Minutes. Will It Eventually Explode?
  Astronomers have found a Long Period Radio Transient about 5,000 light years away that's different from other LPTs. Other LPTs have a white dwarf and a red dwarf, but this one has a white dwarf and a main sequence star. The exact cause of the binary pair's radio pulses isn't certain, but this work narrows it down. It also shows that these binaries could be common progenitors for Type Ia supernova explosions.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/this-rare-binary-star-emits-radio-pulses-every-few-minutes-will-it-eventually-explode
- Sun, 15 Nov 2026 13:22:00 +0000 — Navigating Lunar Orbit Using Nothing But Shadows And A Power Meter
  Humanity is quickly starting to saturate our ability to track all of the satellites we have in deep space. NASA’s Deep Space Network (DSN) has been the workhorse for tracking such things for decades, but is quickly being overwhelmed by the number of requests it receives. Which in turn makes it harder to track some of the missions that have been launched recently. A new paper, available in pre-print on arXiv, from Ian Down, James McElreath, and Manoranjan Majji, of Advanced Space, Space Exploration Engineering, and Texas A&amp;M respectively, offer a no-cost solution to that tracking problem - use solar array data from a spacecraft’s eclipse to determine where it is.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/navigating-lunar-orbit-using-nothing-but-shadows-and-a-power-meter

  The Cosmic Noon was when the Universe's Stelliferous Era Began, and star formation was prodigious during that epoch. - Fri, 13 Nov 2026 17:10:44 +0000 — Shape-Shifting Robotic "Slugs" Could One Day Crawl Across Alien Worlds
  Robots have been a mainstay of our early space exploration efforts. But they normally have wheels. Or in some cases, rotors. While those solutions are great on flat ground, they struggle with jagged boulders or tight crevices.   It’s always worth being vigilant when skywatching in mid- to late November. Every year, a few Leonid meteors punctuate the November morning sky around the 17th. These silent harbingers are only a hint at the shower’s true storm potential, which culminates once every three decades. Collaboration Seeks to Expand Space Access for Purpose-Driven Leaders. TUSTIN, Calif., November 5, 2026 – Virgin Galactic Holdings, Inc. (NYSE: SPCE) today announced a three-year collaboration with Space for Humanity , a 501(c)(3) non-profit organization dedicated to democratizing access to space. Under this agreement, Virgin Galactic plans to fly three dedicated Space for Humanity spaceflight charters, each with a six-seat cabin, for up to 18 astronauts in total. Space for Humanity may also reconfigure any flight’s cabin, replacing one or more seats with a specialized rack for research experiments.   La Nación / Alliana acuerda con empresarios crear un gabinete de impulso económico El presidente de la República, Pedro Alliana, recibió este lunes a miembros de los gremios empresariales y de la producción con quienes acordó impulsar un espacio de coordinación público-privada . La agenda contempla reformas económicas, revisión de programas sociales y ajustes presupuestarios. En ese marco, se propone la creación de un Gabinete de Impulso Económico Público – Privado. Con este planteamiento, el Gobierno de Pedro Alliana busca instalar una nueva plataforma de trabajo con los principales gremios empresariales, con compromisos de revisión, ejecución y medición de resultados . ### BRA

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Mon, 16 Nov 2026 18:42:00 -0300 — EBC inaugura nova sede em São Paulo e amplia estrutura de produção
  A Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC) inaugurou oficialmente, nesta segunda-feira (16), sua nova sede em São Paulo, localizada na Avenida Prestes Maia, nº 733, na região central da capital paulista. A cerimônia marcou o início de uma nova etapa da atuação da empresa na maior cidade do Brasil, com uma estrutura modernizada, novos espaços de produção e perspectivas de fortalecimento da infraestrutura de transmissão. Notícias relacionadas: EBC recebe selo Diamante em avaliação de transparência pública. EBC conquista mais R$ 23 milhões para investimento na TV 3.0 no Brasil. Na ocasião, houve o descerramento da placa de inauguração da nova sede, um tour de apresentação das novas instalações e a apresentação da banda Som dos Quintos. Participaram da solenidade a diretora-presidente, Antonia Pell
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/geral/noticia/2026-11/ebc-inaugura-nova-sede-em-sao-paulo-e-amplia-estrutura-de-producao

#### Agência iNFRA (press, pt, newsroom)

- undated — Veículos elétricos terão demanda equivalente à geração de Angra em 2036
  Veículos elétricos terão demanda equivalente à geração de Angra em 2036 - Agência iNFRA Ir para o conteúdo 16/11/2026 | 16h06 &nbsp;•&nbsp; Atualização: 16/11/2026 | 17h15 Veículos elétricos terão demanda equivalente à geração de Angra em 2036 Foto: José Cruz/Agência Brasil da Agência iNFRA A frota de veículos elétricos no Brasil deve demandar 14,8 TWh (terawatts-hora) de energia por ano a partir de 2036, segundo projeção da EPE (Empresa de Pesquisa Energética). O volume representa uma taxa média de crescimento de 27% ao ano e equivale à geração anual combinada das usinas nucleares Angra 1 e Angra 2. Os dados constam no caderno de Eletromobilidade divulgado nesta segunda-feira (16) e que vai compor o PDE 2036 (Plano Decenal de Expansão de Energia). A EPE ressalta que esse cenário requer alinhamento da expansão da geração, transmissão e distribuição de energia elétrica. Além disso, aponta
  https://agenciainfra.com/blog/veiculos-eletricos-terao-demanda-equivalente-a-geracao-de-angra-em-2036/

- undated — Exclusive: SpaceComputer Books a D-Orbit Demo Mission with Aptos Orbital in 2027
  Exclusive: SpaceComputer Books a D-Orbit Demo Mission with Aptos Orbital in 2027 Skip to content A new security standard is headed to orbit. SpaceComputer, the Singapore-based in-orbit digital security startup, will fly its Space Fabric secure-compute payload aboard a D-Orbit ION mission next year. The mission aims to test the company’s ability to add a security layer into a satcom network. How it works: SpaceComputer’s Space Fabric sits on top of a satellite’s compute module and uses cryptographic methods to provide verifiable proof that compute processes run in-orbit were, in fact, run in-orbit. The module allows for greater security guarantees regarding space-based data operations, and opens the door for multiple digital payloads to safely operate on the same in-orbit compute module—without being exposed to one another. On the D-Orbit mission, Space Fabric will connect directly to Apt
  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-spacecomputer-books-a-d-orbit-demo-mission-in-2027/

- undated — Starship IFT-15 is in Orbit — Will it be “Caught” When It Returns to Earth?
  Starship IFT-15 is in Orbit &#8212; Will it be &#8220;Caught&#8221; When It Returns to Earth? &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com SpaceX launched the 15th Starship Integrated Flight Test (IFT-15) early this evening from Starbase, Texas. Plans to bring the first stage Booster back to the launch tower for a &#8220;catch&#8221; minutes after liftoff were scrubbed, but hopes remain high that there will be a catch tomorrow when the second stage, Starship, returns from orbit. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . #### MEDIA INAF (science, it, newsroom)

- undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

#### Officiële bekendmakingen (Dutch Government Gazette and parliamentary papers) (government, nl, text-reader)

- undated — Ruimtevaartbeleid; Brief regering; Verslag Ministeriële Conferentie ESA 2025 en beleidsevaluatie ruimtevaartbeleid 2017-2024
  Aan de Voorzitter van de Tweede Kamer der Staten-Generaal Den Haag, 27 januari 2026 Met deze brief informeer ik uw Kamer over de uitkomsten van de Ministeriële Conferentie 2025 (MC25) van de European Space Agency (ESA), die op 26 en 27 november 2025 in Bremen (Duitsland) heeft plaatsgevonden. Ik stuur deze uitkomsten mede namens de Minister van Onderwijs, Cultuur en Wetenschap en de Minister van Defensie. Daarnaast stuur ik uw Kamer een kabinetsreactie op de uitgevoerde beleidsevaluatie van het civiele ruimtevaartbeleid van 2017 tot en met 2024. De Minister van Economische Zaken,V.P.G.Karremans **Uitkomst MC25** De uitkomst van MC25 laat zien dat Europese landen ruimtevaart nadrukkelijk zien als een strategische randvoorwaarde. In de huidige geopolitieke context speelt de ruimte een steeds grotere rol voor onze veiligheid en weerbaarheid, terwijl satellietinfrastructuur en satellietdata ook voor onze samenleving vitaal zijn: voor logistiek, landbouw, (veilige) communicatie, weersvoorspellingen, klimaatonderzoek en dijkbewaking. Tegen deze achtergrond is de Ministeriële Conferentie 2025 (MC25) in Bremen succesvol verlopen. De ESA-lidstaten hebben afspraken gemaakt over een pakket va
  https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-24446-101.html

### CHE

#### Blick (press, de, newsroom)

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

  --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ). #### Monaco Tribune (press, fr, newsroom)

- undated — la création du Bureau des Affaires Spatiales en 2021
  Monaco à la conquête de l&#039;espace --> Sections --> Découvrir Monaco Célébrités Culture Évènements Informations Locales Monaco Dans Le Monde Sortir Structures Sportives Tourisme --> Rejoignez nos 150 000 followers ! Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte
  https://www.monaco-tribune.com/2021/10/lespace-nouveau-centre-dinteret-economique-pour-monaco/

#### Kosmonautix.cz (space_press, cs, newsroom)

- undated — Vulcan selhal při startu s družicemi Amazon Leo
  Vulcan selhal při startu s&nbsp;družicemi Amazon Leo &#8211; Kosmonautix.cz Přeskočit na obsah Vulcan selhal při startu s&nbsp;družicemi Amazon Leo Zdeněk Jánský 16. listopadu 2026 15:00 16. 11. 2026 4 komentáře 4 3 min čtení Při nedělním startu rakety Vulcan s&nbsp;družicemi Amazon Leo došlo k&nbsp;anomálii a&nbsp;mise skončila neúspěchem. Jde o&nbsp;první úplné selhání nosné rakety patřící United Launch Alliance od založení společnosti v&nbsp;roce 2006. Při misi Amazon Leo LV-01 tedy byla přerušena téměř dvacetiletá série startů raket této firmy bez nehody. Nákladem nedělního startu bylo 40 družic pro telekomunikační konstelaci Amazon Leo. Vulcan poprvé letěl v&nbsp;nejsilnější konfiguraci VC6L se šesti pomocnými motory GEM 63XL na tuhé pohonné látky a&nbsp;prodlouženým aerodynamickým krytem. Byla také poprvé použita zkrácená varianta horního stupně Centaur V&nbsp;optimalizovaná pro mi
  https://kosmonautix.cz/2026/11/16/vulcan-selhal-pri-startu-s-druzicemi-amazon-leo/
- undated — NASA rozbíhá novou iniciativu k dopravě vzorků z Marsu
  NASA rozbíhá novou iniciativu k&nbsp;dopravě vzorků z&nbsp;Marsu &#8211; Kosmonautix.cz Přeskočit na obsah NASA rozbíhá novou iniciativu k&nbsp;dopravě vzorků z&nbsp;Marsu Dušan Majer 16. listopadu 2026 0:10 16. 11. 2026 10 komentářů 10 4 min čtení Dlouhodobým cílem NASA je mít schopnost roboticky dopravit na Zemi vzorky z&nbsp;rudé planety. Agentura proto rozbíhá novou iniciativu komplikacemi provázeného programu MSR (Mars Sample Return), která nese označení PSRA (Perseverance Sample Retrieval Architecture). Plán počítá se vznikem čerstvé strategie NASA na získání vzorků odebraných roverem Perseverance a&nbsp;jejich dopravu na Zemi. Iniciativa PSRA byla zmíněna 9.&nbsp;listopadu z&nbsp;úst Tiffany Morgan, ředitelky programu MEP (Mars Exploration Program) agentury NASA, během virtuálního setkání skupiny MEPAG (Mars Exploration Program Analysis Group). - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. #### Space24 (space_press, pl, newsroom)

- undated — Awaria rakiety Vulcan. 40 satelitów Amazon Leo utraconych
  Awaria rakiety Vulcan. 40 satelitów Amazon Leo utraconych Menu Start przeprowadziła firma United Launch Alliance (ULA) z Przylądka Canaveral na Florydzie. Był to piąty lot rakiety Vulcan od jej debiutu w styczniu 2024 r. i zarazem pierwszy od lutego 2026 r., kiedy problemy z jednym z silników pomocniczych doprowadziły do czasowego wstrzymania kolejnych startów. Musk: Starship za kilka tygodni będzie gotów do kolejnej misji Około 10 minut po rozpoczęciu lotu doszło do anomalii, która uniemożliwiła prawidłowe wykonanie misji. ULA potwierdziła, że rakieta nie zdołała umieścić ładunku na docelowej orbicie . Według wstępnych informacji silniki pomocnicze podczas tej misji działały prawidłowo, a anomalia wystąpiła już po ich odłączeniu. Dostępne dane wskazują, że problem mógł pojawić się podczas pracy górnego stopnia Centaur, jednak ULA nie potwierdziła dotąd takiej diagnozy. Była to pierwsza
  https://space24.pl/pojazdy-kosmiczne/systemy-nosne/awaria-rakiety-vulcan-40-satelitow-amazon-leo-utraconych
- undated — Sonda kosmiczna Vigil będzie wyposażona w polskie anteny
  Sonda kosmiczna Vigil będzie wyposażona w polskie anteny Menu Sonda Vigil (z łac. strażnik) to pierwszy europejski satelita do monitorowania pogody kosmicznej. Z głębokiej przestrzeni kosmicznej między Ziemią a Słońcem ( w punkcie Lagrange’a L5 oddalonym o 150 mln kilometrów od Ziemi ) będzie wykrywać potencjalnie niebezpieczne zdarzenia słoneczne, zanim będą widoczne z Ziemi. Awaria rakiety Vulcan. 40 satelitów Amazon Leo utraconych Na stronie Europejskiej Agencji Kosmicznej (ESA) wyjaśniono, że przekazywanie danych z Vigil do sieci Space Weather Service Network , prowadzonej przez Biuro Pogody Kosmicznej ESA, zapewni operatorom sieci satelitarnych i energetycznych, systemów telekomunikacyjnych oraz agencji kosmicznych czas na ochronę infrastruktury na Ziemi, satelitów na orbicie okołoziemskiej i astronautów na ISS. Jak podano w komunikacie nadesłanym w poniedziałek z Sener Polska, firm
  https://space24.pl/przemysl/sektor-krajowy/sonda-kosmiczna-vigil-bedzie-wyposazona-w-polskie-anteny
- undated — Satelity ICEYE z rozdzielczością 11 cm. Firma prezentuje Gen5
  Satelity ICEYE z rozdzielczością 11 cm. Firma prezentuje Gen5 Menu 16 listopada 2026 r. firma ICEYE zaprezentowała Gen5 – komercyjny system satelitarny wyposażony w radar z syntetyczną aperturą (SAR) o najwyższej rozdzielczości dostępnej na rynku. Z przekazanych informacji wynika, że odbiorcom rządowym Gen5 umożliwia obrazowanie przy szerokości pasma 1800 MHz, co pozwala uzyskać rozdzielczość nawet 11 cm , w granicach dopuszczonych przepisami Międzynarodowego Związku Telekomunikacyjnego (ITU). „Dzięki temu analitycy mogą z większą pewnością wykrywać i identyfikować obiekty mniejsze niż dotąd, niezależnie od pory doby i warunków pogodowych. Obraz sytuacji staje się bardziej wiarygodny, a decyzje kluczowe dla powodzenia misji zapadają na podstawie pełniejszych informacji” – podkreśla firma ICEYE.   https://space24.pl/przemysl/sektor-krajowy/satelity-iceye-z-rozdzielczoscia-11-cm-firma-prezentuje-gen5

Dogodek je namenjen izmenjavi dobrih praks in krepitvi sodelovanja na področju politik zaposlovanja in trga dela.   Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo | GOV.SI Domov Področja Podjetništvo in gospodarstvo Inovativnost in tehnološki razvoj Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo Slovenija je od 1. januarja 2025 polnopravna članica Evropske vesoljske agencije (angleško The European Space Agency – Esa), kar slovenskim podjetjem in razvojnoraziskovalnim institucijam omogoča sodelovanje pri projektih Ese na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Sodelujejo lahko podjetja in institucije, ki se ukvarjajo z inovativnimi materiali, tehnologijami, procesi ali informacijskimi rešitvami. Priložnost za sodelovanje z Eso imajo tudi mala in srednja podjetja, kar potrjujejo uspešni projekti podjetij iz Slovenije, ki so to priložnost že izkoristila.   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje. Sonda Rosetta je svoje potovanje začela leta 2004, avgusta letos pa se je uspela utiriti v orbito kometa, ki s hitrostjo 65.000 kilomet
  https://www.zurnal24.si/svet/clovek-je-prvic-poslal-sondo-na-komet-239670

Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. - undated — News | Turkmen hemrasy
  In April 2025, Turkmenistan will celebrate an important date in its modern history the 10th anniversary of the launch of the first national communications satellite, “TurkmenAlem 52.0E”. This event was an important step towards the development of telecommunications technologies in Turkmenistan, strengthening the country's international position in the field of communications and notification, as well as opening up new opportunities for scientific and technological progress. On April 28, 2015, the first Turkmen communications satellite was successfully launched into space aboard a “Falcon 9” rocket from Cape Canaveral (Florida, USA state). The device was created by the French company “Thales Alenia Space” by order of Turkmenistan. This satellite has become not only a technical breakthrough, but also a symbol of a new era of modernization and international cooperation. The “TurkmenAlem 52.0E” is a unique device with high technical characteristics. It provides satellite telephony, data transmission and other types of communications, covering vast territories of Central Asia, Europe, the Middle East and North Africa.br> With the launch of the “TurkmenAlem 52.0E” satellite, Turkmenistan
  https://turkmenhemrasy.gov.tm/en/news/1338

  ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. Establishment of the Agency (1)There is hereby established a state corporation to be known as the Kenya Space Agency.(2)The Agency shall be a body corporate with perpetual succession and a common seal and shall, in its corporate name, be capable of—(a)suing and being sued;(b)taking, purchasing or otherwise acquiring, holding, charging or disposing of movable and immovable property;(c)receiving, investing, borrowing and lending money; and(d)doing or performing any such other thing or act, including entering into such contracts as may be
  https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

  Nzenzele : la place des fêtes offerte par feu Jules Esdras laissée à l’abandon | Gabonmediatime.com | Actualités Gabon Facebook X Linkedin Tumblr Pinterest Reddit WhatsApp Telegram Partager par email Imprimer Ajouter GabonMediaTime à vos sources préférées Média certifié par Média Métri Une vue de la place des fêtes de Nzenzelé © D.R. Ecouter l'article À Nzenzele, la dégradation avancée de la place des fêtes offerte à la communauté par feu l’honorable Jules Esdras suscite des interrogations sur l’entretien des infrastructures collectives. Selon un témoignage publié ce lundi 16 novembre 2026, l’édifice présente plusieurs signes de détérioration : toiture partiellement arrachée, dalle inondée, murs dégradés et végétation envahissante. Pourtant régulièrement utilisé pour les célébrations nationales et les rassemblements communautaires, cet espace symbolique semble aujourd’hui privé d’un entr
  https://gabonmediatime.com/nzenzele-la-place-des-fetes-offerte-par-feu-jules-esdras-laissee-a-labandon/

#### Royal Centre for Remote Sensing (CRTS) (space_office, fr, text-reader)

- undated — Centre Royal de Télédétection Spatiale
  ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma

- undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. - undated — Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully
  Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far.   โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา วิสัยทัศน์อนาคตด้านอวกาศ ครอบคลุมทั้งการปกป้องโลกให้ดีขึ้น การนำประโยชน์จากอวกาศมาใช้งานในโลก รวมทั้งการแสวงหาความเป็นไปได้อื่นๆ ที่มนุษย์ยังไปไม่ถึง ทิศทางอนาคต จากผู้นำเอกชนระดับโลกด้านอวกาศอย่าง &ldquo; อีลอน มัสก์ &rdquo; ผู้ก่อตั้ง บริษัทสเปซเอ็กซ์ (SpaceX) วางวิสัยทัศน์ไปสู่การตั้งถิ่นฐานของมนุษย์โลกที่ดาวอังคาร ในขณะที่ผู้นำภาคธุรกิจอย่าง &ldquo; เจฟฟ์ เบซอส &rdquo; จาก บริษัทบลู ออริจิน (Blue Origin) มีแผนอนาคตที่จะทำให้ดวงจันทร์เป็นอาณานิคมแห่งใหม่ของโลก เพื่อตอบโจทย์ข้อท้าทายที่โลกไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้ จากวิสัยทัศน์อนาคตของผู้นำระดับโลกเหล่านี้ เป็นกุญแจสำคัญในการต่อยอดโอกาสของ เศรษฐกิจอวกาศที่นานาประเทศจับตามอง 5 แนวทาง ได้แก่ 1. การเดินทางสู่อวกาศ (Going to space)

- undated — Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year
  Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. A House committee on space affairs is calling for more research funding and international collaboration to boost the number of Thai aerospace engineers. Bhumjaithai Party MP Settapong Malisuwan, chairman of the subcommittee on space economy and security, said development of Thailand&#39;s space sector is being hampered by lack of international collaboration and transparency. - undated — Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’
  Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ In Focus Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ MONDAY, SEPTEMBER 05, 2022 | THE NATION สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added.   A popular garden in central Seoul is trading autumn jazz for Christmas lights, hot snacks and handmade gifts. Sejong Center for the Performing Arts said Tuesday that Curtain Call Garden will reopen as a winter market from Tuesday through Jan. 3 in its Art Garden courtyard, operating daily from 11 a.m. to 9 p.m. The seasonal transformation will bring traditional winter treats, warm drinks, crafts and holiday decorations to the space, which opened in October as part of Seoul's initiative to stimulate nighttime economic activity. Visitors can buy fish-shaped pastries, caramel-filled fried doughnuts, roasted sweet potatoes and savory fish cake from Happy Carp Bread, while Tea Tree Vin Chaud will serve warm mulled wine and highballs.
